  New Archetypes From

<h1 style="text-align:center; font-style:italic; margin:0px; padding:0px;">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">With the arrival of Evolving Skies this August, we’ll have a handful of new deck archetypes joining the fray—and with rotation, the meta will be shaken up quite thoroughly. As always, some deck archetypes are better than others—some are meme tier and some are merely playable, fun decks without much competitive promise. Lists below are very rough drafts and are intended as a starting place for testing, not as tournament-ready deck lists. Consider using a proxy tool like the one offered by <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/tools/proxies/">LimitlessTCG</a> to test prior to making any purchases.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Below is a list of archetypes that may be reasonably playable after this year’s rotation—archetypes from competitive standouts to enjoyable casual archetypes are listed below.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Archetypes New to Evolving Skies</strong></p>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_75167"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Leafeon V and use its Greening Cells ability early to attach a Grass Energy to prepare for Leafeon VMAX to use its Grass Knot attack, increasing the damage it deals with Galar Mine and, for Pokémon with retreat cost reducing tools attached to them, Tool Jammer.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/eeveeheroes/002.shtml">Leafeon V EVS 7</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/eeveeheroes/003.shtml">Leafeon VMAX EVS 8</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/rcl/160/">Galar Mine</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/eeveeheroes/063.shtml">Snow Leaf Badge</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">The <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/147/">Cinccino</a> engine in place of the Inteleon engine.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_68470"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18</strong><br>4 Leafeon V EVS 7<br>3 Leafeon VMAX EVS 8<br>3 Sobble CRE 41<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 33 </strong><br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Bird Keeper DAA 159<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>3 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Snow Leaf Badge EVS 159<br>2 Tool Jammer BST 136<br>1 Energy Search SSH 161<br>4 Galar Mine RCL 160</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 9 </strong><br>6 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1<br>3 Capture Energy RCL 171</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://youtu.be/hO1LZ-kq2XE?t=653">Celio’s Network’s version of this archetype</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=VJs8y3FA75M">OmniPoke’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_125318"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Pile energy onto Maractus using Cherrim's <em>Spring Bloom</em> ability, then fire away using Maractus' <em>Powerful Needles</em> attack and use the effect of Glimwood Tangle to reflip coin flips for <em>Powerful Needles </em>when the flips are especially bad.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/7/">Maractus SSH 7</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/rapidstrikemaster/006.shtml">Cherrim BST 8</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/daa/162/">Glimwood Tangle</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/blueskystream/004.shtml">Tropius EVS 6</a> as a revenge attacker, using its Rally Back attack to deal quick and consistent damage after one of your Pokémon is knocked out. Alternatively, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/9/">Dhelmise V SSH 9</a> as a revenge attacker or secondary attacker.</p></li></ul><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/15/">Shaymin VIV 15</a> as a stalling option, taking advantage of Shaymin’s Flower Bearing attack to slow down your opponent’s setup.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SHF/3/">Celebi SHF 3</a> as a alternate pivot and means of fishing out Twin Energy to up the number of flips on Maractus’ Powerful Needles attack.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_118731"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 14</strong><br>4 Maractus SSH 7<br>3 Cherubi BST 7<br>3 Cherrim BST 8<br>2 Jirachi VIV 119<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104<br>1 Oranguru SSH 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32 </strong><br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Skyla RCL 166<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>3 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Energy Retrieval SSH 160<br>2 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>1 Energy Recycler GRI 123<br>3 Glimwood Tangle DAA 162</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 14 </strong><br>10 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1<br>4 Twin Energy RCL 174</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_28435" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_28435"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Moderate</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Setup a Decidueye against an opposing deck focused around Pokémon V or Pokémon VMAX. Take gradual knockouts using Decidueye’s Splitting Arrow attack and Inteleon CRE’s <em>Quick Shooting</em> ability. Use Scoop Up Net to take away gusting targets—like Snorlax—from your opponent.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/daa/13/">Decidueye DAA 13</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cre/43/">Inteleon CRE 43</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Watch the Archetype in Action</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=sLWSqAQ7HoU">The Sableyes</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=2kjUg-Zkvg0">AzulGG</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=S5a_i7eEODw">Tricky Gym</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_167276"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 19</strong><br>4 Rowlet DAA 11<br>4 Decidueye DAA 13<br>4 Sobble CRE 41<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>1 Snorlax VIV 131</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32</strong><br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Bird Keeper DAA 159<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>1 Raihan EVS 152<br>1 Zinnia's Resolve EVS 164<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Rare Candy SSH 180<br>2 Big Parasol DAA 157<br>2 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>2 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>1 Energy Search SSH 161<br>1 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>1 Tool Jammer BST 136  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 9 </strong><br>5 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1<br>4 Capture Energy RCL 171</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=JoGoDfn_EaM">ZapdosTCG’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_30904" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_30904"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Dhelmise V into Dhelmise VMAX and Grookey into Rillaboom via Rare Candy. Use Rillaboom’s Voltage Beat ability to power up Dhelmise V’s Anchor Anger or Giga Hammer attacks, Dhelmise VMAX’s Swinging Chain or Max Anchor attacks, or Dubwool V’s Revenge Blast attack, as advantageous.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/14/">Rillaboom SSH 14</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/9/">Dhelmise V SSH 9</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SHF/10/">Dhelmise VMAX SHF 10</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/22/">Zarude V VIV 22</a> as an alternate means of acceleration with the ability to heal with its Jungle Rising attack.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_353775"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 17 </strong><br>4 Dhelmise V SSH 9<br>3 Dhelmise VMAX SHF 10<br>3 Grookey SSH 11<br>1 Thwackey SHF 12<br>3 Rillaboom SSH 14<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>1 Dubwool V RCL 153  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 31 </strong><br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Rare Candy SSH 180<br>4 Switch SSH 183<br>3 Great Ball CPA 52<br>2 Vitality Band SSH 185<br>1 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>3 Turffield Stadium CPA 68</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12 </strong><br>10 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1<br>2 Capture Energy RCL 171</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_32495" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_32495"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Non-Competitive</p>

          </div>
        
          
        

        
          
          <figcaption data-sqsp-image-classic-block-caption-container="" class="image-caption-wrapper">
            <div class="image-caption"><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Illus. 5ban Graphics</p></div>
          </figcaption>
        
      
        </figure>
      

    </div>
  


  


</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_418464"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Orbeetle V and Galarian Moltres V, evolving Orbeetle V to Orbeetle VMAX as soon as possible to begin taking advantage of its Eerie Beam ability, which places a damage counter on each of your opponent’s Pokémon when Orbeetle VMAX is in the Active Spot. Use the deck’s switching options including Switch, Bird Keeper, Air Balloon, and Escape Rope to switch between Orbeetle VMAX to build up damage, attacking with Galarian Moltres V as necessary or bench Sableye V and use Energy Switch on an energy attached to Galarian Moltres V via its Direflame Wings ability to get Sableye V’s Crazy Claws attack powered up the same turn the Sableye V is benched. </p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/21/">Orbeetle VMAX VIV 21</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/120/">Sableye V SSH 120</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cre/97/">Galarian Moltres V CRE 97</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Giving up on Orbeetle VMAX entirely and switching to a Flapple-focused damage spreading engine like the one featured in the <a href="#sableyev">Sableye V</a> deck list on this page.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_425517"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 17</strong><br>3 Orbeetle V VIV 20<br>3 Orbeetle VMAX VIV 21<br>3 Sableye V SSH 120<br>2 Galarian Moltres V CRE 97<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>2 Galarian Zigzagoon SSH 117<br>1 Galarian Zapdos V CRE 80<br>1 Ditto V SHF 50</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 33</strong><br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Bird Keeper DAA 159<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Energy Switch SSH 162<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Switch SSH 183<br>3 Great Ball CPA 52<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Exp. Share BST 126<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125<br>3 Turffield Stadium CPA 68</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10</strong><br>8 Darkness Energy SWSHEnergy 7<br>2 Aurora Energy SSH 186</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5ExoprRjM_c">ZapdosTCG’s version of post-rotation Orbeetle VMAX, featuring Jolteon VMAX</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_26762" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_26762"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. </div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_467428"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Use Level Ball and Quick Ball to get Charmander and Minccino set up as quickly as possible, searching for Rare Candy and Evolution Incense with Cinccino’s Make Do Ability to setup Charizard—all the while discarding Leon with Make Do, Quick Ball, Charizard’s own Battle Sense ability. Use manual attachments and Raihan, as necessary, to power up Charizard’s Royal Blaze attack, using it to take knockouts against the opponent’s Pokémon. Gust as necessary using Boss’s Orders to select knockouts.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/25/">Charizard VIV 25</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/147/">Cinccino SSH 147</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/154/">Leon</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/150/">Peony</a> as a very aggressive supporter that can seek out specific cards you need while discarding your hand to ensure that Leon finds its way into the discard pile. In addition to Peony, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/169/">Rose Tower</a> and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/6/">Kricketune V BST 6</a> to ensure your hand isn’t dead after using Peony.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/121/">Bruno</a> and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/65/">Zinnia’s Resolve</a> as alternate draw supporter options.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/131/">Snorlax VIV 131</a> as an early-game draw option and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/104/">Crobat V</a> as an additional option to draw additional cards.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">A thin 1-1 or 2-2 line of <a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/skyscrapingperfect/040.shtml">Altaria EVS 106</a> to seek out Leon as discard fodder or other supporters to use, as needed.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/126/">Exp. Share</a> to preserve energy attachments on Charizard.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_456133"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 16<br></strong>4 Charmander VIV 23<br>1 Charmeleon VIV 24<br>4 Charizard VIV 25<br>3 Minccino SSH 145<br>3 Cinccino SSH 147<br>1 Oranguru SSH 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 34</strong><br>4 Leon VIV 154<br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Raihan EVS 152<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Rare Candy SSH 180<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10</strong><br>10 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://youtu.be/w4CSMGa4JQM?t=157">The Sableyes’ version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_35717" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_35717"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Flareon V into Flareon VMAX and attach requisite energy to it, lowering the cost of its Max Explosion attack with Elemental Badge. Take knockouts with Max Explosion, which discards the top 5 cards of your deck and does 100 damage for each Energy card the attack discards. Bench and use Galarian Moltres V’s Direflame Wings ability to pull Darkness Energy discarded with Max Explosion onto Moltres to power up its Aura Burn attack.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/vmaxspecialseteeveeheroes/001.shtml">Flareon VMAX EVS 18</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/eeveeheroes/062.shtml">Elemental Badge EVS 147</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/141/">Single Strike Energy</a> to boost Flareon VMAX’s damage or Heat Fire Energy to boost its HP.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/124/">Energy Recycler</a> to recycle discarded Fire energy.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/15/">Volcarona V</a> as a potent late-game attacker that also recycles the deck’s basic Energy cards for reuse.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_487722"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 10</strong><br>4 Flareon V EVS 169<br>3 Flareon VMAX EVS 18<br>2 Galarian Moltres V CRE 97<br>1 Kricketune V BST 6</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 21 </strong><br>4 Peony CRE 150<br>1 Rose DAA 168<br>4 Pokégear 3.0 SSH 174<br>3 Elemental Badge EVS 147<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>2 Rose Tower DAA 169</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 29 </strong><br>11 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2<br>6 Darkness Energy SWSHEnergy 7<br>4 Aurora Energy SSH 186<br>4 Capture Energy RCL 171<br>4 Treasure Energy EVS 165</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_36539" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_36539"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Moderate</p>

PLANETA Mochizuki" elementtiming="system-image-block" data-sqsp-image-classic-block-image="" src="/images/a35085c36d1d7fba.webp" width="550" height="294" sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, (max-width: 767px) 50vw, 50vw" style="display:block;object-fit: cover; width: 100%; height: 100%; object-position: 50% 50%" srcset="/images/a35085c36d1d7fba.webp 100w, /images/a35085c36d1d7fba.webp 300w, /images/a35085c36d1d7fba.webp 500w, /images/a35085c36d1d7fba.webp 750w, /images/a35085c36d1d7fba.webp 1000w, /images/a35085c36d1d7fba.webp 1500w, /images/a35085c36d1d7fba.webp 2500w" loading="lazy" decoding="async" data-loader="sqs">

            </div>

          <figcaption data-sqsp-image-classic-block-caption-container="" class="image-caption-wrapper">

          </figcaption>

    </div>
  


  


</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_605063"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Victini V into Victini VMAX and use a combination of manually attaching Fire Energy and Rose to power up your Victini VMAX as the opportunity presents, refilling your hand with Kricketune V’s Exciting Stage ability or with Rose Tower. Use Victini VMAX’s Max Victory attack to take knockouts, dealing 100 damage by default or 220 damage if your opponent’s Active Pokémon is a Pokémon V (including Pokémon VMAX). Use Umbreon VMAX to gust your opponent’s Pokémon when unable to use Boss’s Orders (because of needing to use another supporter, like Rose).</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/22/">Victini VMAX BST 22</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/48/">Umbreon VMAX EVS 95</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/168/">Rose</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Additional copies of Umbreon V and Umbreon VMAX.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_590237"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Based on a list by <a href="https://twitter.com/PeterJoltik/status/1391186463170170882">Peter Kica</a> and <a href="https://twitter.com/AngelJMiranda">Angel Miranda</a></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 16</strong><br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>2 Umbreon V EVS 94<br>2 Umbreon VMAX EVS 95<br>2 Victini V BST 21<br>2 Victini V SSH 25<br>4 Victini VMAX BST 22<br>1 Eldegoss V RCL 19<br>1 Kricketune V BST 6</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Rose DAA 168<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Pokégear 3.0 SSH 174<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Big Charm SSH 158<br>2 Energy Search SSH 161<br>2 Escape Rope BST 125<br>2 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Rose Tower DAA 169</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12 </strong><br>10 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2<br>2 Heat Fire Energy DAA 174</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Ftl0SYya3Hc">ZapdosTCG’s version of this archetype, featuring the Inteleon engine</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_37315" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_37315"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Minimal</p>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_608204"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Get Scorbunny and Minccino down as quickly as possible to prepare them to evolve. Search for Rare Candy with the deck’s various draw cards to setup Cinderace. Use Cinderace’s <em>Libero </em>ability in combination with Air Balloon, Recycle Energy, and Bird Keeper to guarantee a Flare Striker each turn.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/34/">Cinderace SSH 30</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/183/">Switch SSH 183</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/156/">Air Balloon SSH 156</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/31/">Scorbunny SSH 31</a> in place of some or all of the <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/30/">Scorbunny SSH 30</a>. </p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/63/">Raihan</a> to make it easier to recover when one of the deck’s Pokémon is knocked out.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_577403"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18 </strong><br>4 Scorbunny SSH 30<br>2 Raboot SSH 32<br>4 Cinderace SSH 34<br>3 Minccino SSH 145<br>3 Cinccino SSH 147<br>2 Snorlax VIV 131</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 31 </strong><br>4 Bird Keeper DAA 159<br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Rare Candy SSH 180<br>2 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>1 Fan of Waves BST 127</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11 </strong><br>7 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2<br>4 Capture Energy RCL 171</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1627529113938_920322" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1627529113938_920322"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Non-Competitive</p>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1627529113938_995648"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Use Snorlax’s Gormandize ability on early turns to draw additional cards. Meanwhile, evolve Blastoise V into Blastoise VMAX as early as possible and use a combination of manual attachments and Melony, power-up Blastoise’s Grand Falls attack, which you can in turn use to power up additional benched Blastoise. Use a mix of Blastoise’s Grand Falls and G-Max Bombard attacks to take knockouts, boosting G-Max Bombard’s damage with Telescopic Sight. Use Drizzile and Inteleon SSH’s Shady Dealings ability to help find key cards throughout the game and use Inteleon CRE’s Quick Shooting ability to help set up or finish knockouts on your opponent’s Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSP/103/">Blastoise VMAX SWSH103</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146/">Melony</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/1/">Suicune V EVS 31</a> as an alternate attacker and consistency booster.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/36/">Froslass CRE 36</a> as an additional means of accelerating energy onto Blastoise VMAX.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/160/">Energy Retrieval</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/157/">Nessa</a>, and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/169/">Training Court</a> as an alternative option to Nessa to recovery Energy cards.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1627529113938_933638"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>4 Blastoise V SWSH101<br>3 Blastoise VMAX SWSH103<br>4 Sobble CRE 41<br>4 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>2 Snorlax VIV 131</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 30</strong><br>4 Melony CRE 146<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>2 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>2 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Telescopic Sight VIV 160<br>1 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10</strong><br>10 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_39634" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_39634"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.             <div class="image-caption"><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Illus. 5ban Graphics</p></div>

        </figure>
      

    </div>
  


  


</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_732119"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Lapras V and Snom as early as possible so that they can evolve into Lapras VMAX and Frosmoth, respectively. Using Frosmoth’s Ice Dance and Melony, flood Lapras VMAX with energy to raise the damage of its G-Max Pump attack, which does 90 damage plus 30 damage for each Water energy attached to Lapras VMAX. Over the course of the game, bench Sobble and evolve them into Drizzile to take advantage of its Shady Dealings ability, which allows you to find any Trainer card you need and evolve the Drizzile as necessary into Inteleon CRE to take advantage of its Quick Shooting ability to reduce the number of Energy necessary to take knock outs.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/50/">Lapras VMAX SSH 50</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/64/">Frosmoth SSH 64</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146/">Melony</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/1/">Suicune V EVS 31</a> as an alternate attacker and consistency booster.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/160/">Energy Retrieval</a> and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/169/">Training Court</a> as an alternative option to Nessa to recovery Energy cards.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_716081"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 21</strong><br>3 Lapras V SSH 49<br>3 Lapras VMAX SSH 50<br>3 Snom SHF 29<br>2 Frosmoth SSH 64<br>3 Sobble CRE 41<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 29 </strong><br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Melony CRE 146<br>1 Nessa VIV 157<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10</strong><br>10 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_27450" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_27450"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Non-Competitive</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Horsea, using Rare Candy to evolve them into Kingdra as soon as possible. Meanwhile, bench and evolve Snom into Frosmoth, flooding a benched Kingdra with energy to move it into the Active Spot to attack using its Aqua Burst attack. As Kingdra are knocked out, preserve the attached energy with Kingdra’s Deep Sea King ability and recycle the Horsea and Kingdra using Nessa.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/rapidstrikemaster/019.shtml">Kingdra BST 33</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/64/">Frosmoth SSH 64</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/rcl/156/">Capacious Bucket</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/148/">Oranguru SSH 148</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/1/">Suicune V EVS 31,</a> and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cpa/64/">Rotom Phone</a> as a consistency booster that can make it easier to find key cards, like Rare Candy.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/169/">Training Court</a> or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/171/">Ordinary Rod</a> as additional recovery options, especially for energy.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/168/">Tool Scrapper</a> to remove especially problematic tools, like those that boost the HP of your opponent’s Pokémon.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_847662"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18</strong><br>4 Horsea BST 31<br>1 Seadra BST 32<br>4 Kingdra BST 33<br>3 Snom SHF 29<br>3 Frosmoth SSH 64<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>1 Suicune DAA 37</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 31 </strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Nessa VIV 157<br>1 Raihan EVS 152<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Rare Candy SSH 180<br>3 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Switch SSH 183  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11 </strong><br>11 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_41123" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_41123"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. </div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_884585"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Wailord V, Snom, and Minccino, evolving Snom into Frosmoth and Minccino into Cinccino as soon as possible. Use Cinccino’s Make Do, Professor’s Research, and Quick Ball to quick get Water energy cards into the discard pile to make use of Wailord V’s Draw Up attack, which will attach three of them directly to itself from there. Attach Cape of Toughness to Wailord V to make it even more difficult to knock out. With Glimwood Tangle in play, use Wailord V’s Ocean Waves attack, which does 120 damage times the number of heads flipped out of three coin flips.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CPA/13/">Wailord V CPA 13</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/160/">Cape of Toughness</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/162/">Glimwood Tangle</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146/">Melony</a> as an additional means of getting enough energy onto Wailord V so that it can use its Ocean Waves attack.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_868016"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 16<br></strong>4 Wailord V CPA 13<br>3 Snom SHF 29<br>2 Frosmoth SSH 64<br>3 Minccino SSH 145<br>3 Cinccino SSH 147<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 34 <br></strong>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Bird Keeper DAA 159<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>3 Cape of Toughness DAA 160<br>3 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Energy Retrieval SSH 160<br>3 Glimwood Tangle DAA 162 </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10 <br></strong>10 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1627948496993_660285" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1627948496993_660285"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Minimal</p>

          <figcaption data-sqsp-image-classic-block-caption-container="" class="image-caption-wrapper">
            <div class="image-caption"><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Illus. PLANETA Tsuji</p></div>
          </figcaption>
        
      
        </figure>
      

    </div>
  


  


</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1627948496993_731884"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Going second, start with Glaceon V in the Active Spot and use its Ice Ascension attack to immediately evolve it into Glaceon VMAX, whose ability blocks the attacks of Pokémon VMAX. Play down Minccino and evolve it into Cinccino to take advantage of its Make Do ability as early as possible and evolve Snorunt into Froslass as necessary to take advantage of its Frost Over ability, which attaches a Water energy from your discard pile to one of your Pokémon in play. Take gradual spread damage knockouts using Glaceon VMAX’s Max Icile, using Boss’s Orders to gust up vulnerable targets when the opportunity presents. Use Dyna Tree Hill to prevent healing and to remove the hostile stadium Path to the Peak, which can also be removed with Pumpkaboo’s Pumpkin Pit ability.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/25/">Glaceon VMAX EVS 41</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/16/">Pumpkaboo EVS 76</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146/">Melony</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/154/">Leon</a> to give a quick boost to Glaceon VMAX’s damage when necessary.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/1/">Suicune V</a> as an option to help counter the Pokémon V that will be used against Glaceon to get around its Crystal Veil ability.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1627948496993_685995"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 19</strong><br>4 Glaceon V EVS 40<br>3 Glaceon VMAX EVS 41<br>3 Minccino SSH 145<br>3 Cinccino SSH 147<br>2 Snorunt CRE 35<br>2 Froslass CRE 36<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104<br>1 Pumpkaboo EVS 76</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32</strong><br>4 Melony CRE 146<br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>2 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>2 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>2 Snow Leaf Badge EVS 159<br>1 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>3 Dyna Tree Hill CRE 135</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 9</strong><br>9 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=nXUaPEHjVgw">OmniPoke’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_41954" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_41954"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Minimal</p>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_944767"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play Keldeo V and play down Snom as early as possible so that they can evolve into Frosmoth. Using Frosmoth’s Ice Dance and Melony, flood Keldeo V with energy to raise the damage of its Secret Sword attack, which does 50 damage plus 30 damage for each Water energy attached to Keldeo V. Over the course of the game, bench Sobble and evolve them into Drizzile to take advantage of its Shady Dealings ability, which allows you to find any Trainer card you need and evolve the Drizzile as necessary into Inteleon CRE to take advantage of its Quick Shooting ability to reduce the number of Energy necessary to take knock outs.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/53/">Keldeo V SSH 53</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/64/">Frosmoth SSH 64</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146/">Melony</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/1/">Suicune V EVS 31</a> as an alternate attacker and consistency booster.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/160/">Energy Retrieval</a> and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/169/">Training Court</a> as an alternative option to Nessa to recovery Energy cards.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_927199"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 19</strong><br>4 Keldeo V SSH 53<br>3 Sobble CRE 41<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>3 Snom SHF 29<br>2 Frosmoth SSH 64<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 30</strong><br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Melony CRE 146<br>1 Nessa VIV 157<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Great Ball CPA 52<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>2 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11</strong><br>11 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1626786643544_173978" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1626786643544_173978"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Use a Frosmoth’s Ice Dance ability to help get three or more energy on each Wishiwashi in play and use Cape of Toughness to further boost its HP, bringing Wishiwashi’s HP from 30 to a total of 230. Take knockouts using Wishiwashi’s attack, which does 30 damage plus 30 more damage for each basic Energy attached to it. </p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/25/">Wishiwashi EVS 36</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/64/">Frosmoth SSH 64</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/160/">Cape of Toughness</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/59/">Rescue Carrier</a> to be able to quickly reuse both Wishiwashi and the Frosmoth line.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1626786643544_210321"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 16</strong><br>4 Wishiwashi EVS 46<br>3 Minccino SSH 145<br>3 Cinccino SSH 147<br>3 Snom SHF 29<br>3 Frosmoth SSH 64</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Nessa VIV 157<br>4 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>4 Energy Retrieval SSH 160<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Cape of Toughness DAA 160<br>2 Switch SSH 183</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12</strong><br>12 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://twitter.com/JaxuTcg/status/1417039373929955328/photo/1">JaxuTCG’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>

  <h2 style="padding-bottom:0px; margin-bottom:0px;">Inteleon VMAX</h2>
  <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Highly Competitive</p>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_414442"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Inteleon V into Inteleon VMAX and bench Sobble in preparation for using Drizzile’s Shady Dealings ability on subsequent turns. On early turns, use Hydro Snipe attack to slow down your opponent’s setup. As soon as possible, use Melony to power up Inteleon VMAX’s Max Bullet attack and spread damage on your opponent’s most vulnerable benched Pokémon. Use Boss’s Orders to take key knockouts, like critically damaged Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S1a/23/">Inteleon VMAX RCL 50</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S1a/63/">Capacious Bucket</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146/">Melony</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/160/">Telescopic Sight</a> to boost the bench damage of Inteleon VMAX’s Max Bullet attack against benched Pokémon V (including Pokémon VMAX).</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cre/152/">Rugged Helmet</a> to lean further into the energy disruption aspect of the archetype, making it more difficult for some opposing archetypes to chain attacks against Inteleon VMAX.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/126/">Exp. Share</a> to make it easier to get back to attacking with Max Bullet after one of your Inteleon has been knocked out.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_394818"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>4 Inteleon V RCL 49<br>3 Inteleon VMAX RCL 50<br>4 Sobble CRE 41<br>4 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon SSH 58<br>2 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 30</strong><br>4 Melony CRE 146<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>2 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>1 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>3 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10</strong><br>8 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3<br>2 Capture Energy RCL 171</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=FwYokxH9B4U">Celio’s Network’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_28138" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_28138"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. </div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_976956"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Fill your bench and evolve Kubfu as quickly as you can so that each of your Pokémon in play is a Rapid Strike Pokémon. Then, use Rapid Strike Energy to power Rapid Strike Urshifu’s Rapid-Fisted Rush attack, which does 30 damage for each Rapid Strike Pokémon you have in play—up to 180 damage with a single hit. Combine that damage with Inteleon CRE’s Quick Shooting ability to take knockouts or use Boss’s Orders to pick on your opponent’s vulnerable benched Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/matchlessfighter/019.shtml">Rapid Strike Urshifu CRE 44</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/matchlessfighter/018.shtml">Inteleon CRE 43</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/37/">Octillery BST 37</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/140/">Rapid Strike Energy</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S5a/69/">Brawly</a> to quickly bench multiple Rapid Strike Pokémon at once.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Water Energy as a Ordinary Rod-recoverable means of powering up Rapid Strike Urshifu’s attack.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_959093"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 23</strong><br>4 Kubfu CRE 93<br>4 Rapid Strike Urshifu CRE 44<br>4 Sobble CRE 55<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>3 Inteleon CRE 43<br>2 Remoraid BST 36<br>2 Octillery BST 37<br>1 Falinks BST 83</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 28</strong><br>3 Bruno BST 121<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Marnie CPA 56<br>1 Korrina's Focus BST 128<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>3 Rare Candy SSH 180<br>1 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>2 Tower of Waters BST 138  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 9</strong><br>4 Rapid Strike Energy BST 140<br>4 Spiral Energy CRE 159<br>1 Capture Energy RCL 171</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1624771442437_179814" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624771442437_179814"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Highly Competitive</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Ice Rider Calyrex V into Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX, and bench Sobble to prepare it to evolve on subsequent turns to take advantage of Drizzile’s Shady Dealings ability. Use a combination of manually attaching energy and Melony to power up Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX’s two attacks, Ride of the High King and Max Lance, and use those attacks to take knockouts against your opponent’s Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cre/46/">Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX CRE 46</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/146/">Melony</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="http://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/154/">Leon</a> to give a quick boost to Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX’s damage on key turns.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/1/">Suicune V</a> as an basic attacker and as an early-game option when Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX isn’t out fast enough.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624771442437_203312"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 21</strong><br>4 Ice Rider Calyrex V CRE 45<br>3 Ice Rider Calyrex VMAX CRE 46<br>4 Sobble CRE 41<br>4 Drizzile SSH 56<br>3 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 31</strong><br>4 Melony CRE 146<br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>3 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 8</strong><br>8 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://twitter.com/AngelJMiranda/status/1414002861537628168">@AngelJMiranda’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>
