</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1630338873650_254139"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Mareep into Flaaffy and use Pikachu V’s Electrify attack and Flaaffy’s Dynamotor ability to get energy into play. Evolve Pikachu V into Pikachu VMAX and use its G-Max Volt Tackle attack to take knockouts, using Tapu Koko V primarily as a pivot to load up energy with Flaaffy’s Dynamotor ability, as necessary.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/44/">Pikachu VMAX VIV 44</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/27/">Flaaffy EVS 54</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/72/">Tapu Koko V SSH 72</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/51/">Tapu Koko VMAX BST 51</a> as a potential option against acceleration-heavy decks, like Shadow Rider Calyrex VMAX</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/48/">Zapdos VIV 48</a> as a finisher to KO benched Pokémon V (including Pokémon VMAX) after your opponent attempts to save them on the bench by retreating them or switching them out of the Active Spot.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1630338873650_205439"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18<br></strong>4 Mareep DAA 55<br>4 Flaaffy EVS 55<br>3 Pikachu V VIV 43<br>3 Pikachu VMAX VIV 44<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>2 Tapu Koko V SSH 72</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32 </strong><br>3 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Bird Keeper DAA 159<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>3 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>3 Stormy Mountains EVS 161 </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10 </strong><br>10 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_55033" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_55033"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <h2 style="padding-bottom:0px; margin-bottom:0px;">Jolteon VMAX</h2>
  <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Minimal</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Jolteon V into Jolteon VMAX and power it up with a combination of manually attaching Energy and Elemental Badge. Use Jolteon V’s Thunder Spear’s attack, Galarian Zigzagoon’s Headbutt Tantrum ability, and Inteleon CRE’s Quick Shooting ability to set damage on your opponent’s Pokémon to prepare for knockouts and to make the sniping secondary effect of Jolteon VMAX’s Max Thunder Rumble attack, which deals 100 damage to a Benched Pokémon that already has any damage counters on it.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/SP4/3/">Jolteon VMAX EVS 51</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="http://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/43/">Inteleon CRE 43</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/117/">Galarian Zigzagoon SSH 117</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Some combination of <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/25/">Glaceon VMAX</a>, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/63/">Snow Leaf Badge</a>, and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/125/">Escape Rope</a> to use as a pivot and wall against Pokémon VMAX,</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_998978"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 19</strong><br>4 Jolteon V EVS 177<br>3 Jolteon VMAX EVS 51<br>3 Sobble CRE 41<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>2 Galarian Zigzagoon SSH 117<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 31 </strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Cheryl BST 123<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Elemental Badge EVS 147<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>3 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Path to the Peak CRE 148</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10 </strong><br>4 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4<br>4 Speed Lightning Energy RCL 173<br>2 Capture Energy RCL 171</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=Cgeg4pUArRI">Tablemon’s version of this archetype</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=5ExoprRjM_c">ZapdosTCG’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1629934129742_326432" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1629934129742_326432"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Mareep into Flaaffy and use Flaaffy’s Dynamotor ability to power up the deck’s attackers, especially Zekrom. Use Zekrom’s Wild Shock to take knockouts and to make it more difficult for your opponent to knock out Zekrom in return because of its Paralysis effect. When advantageous, use Zapdos’ Thunder Snipe attack to take down benched Pokémon V (including VMAX) or Regieleki’s Teraspark to either set up knockouts on your opponent’s Benched Pokémon or to take the knockouts on damaged Pokémon on your opponent’s bench.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/27/">Flaaffy EVS 55</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/60/">Zekrom VIV 57</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/48/">Zapdos VIV 48</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/evs/60/">Regieleki EVS 60</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/48/">Jirachi VIV 119</a> as a more constant pivot and draw option, in place of or in addition to Snorlax.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1629934129742_425556"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18</strong><br>4 Mareep DAA 55<br>4 Flaaffy EVS 55<br>4 Zekrom VIV 57<br>2 Snorlax VIV 131<br>2 Zapdos VIV 48<br>1 Regieleki EVS 60<br>1 Galarian Zigzagoon SSH 117</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32 </strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Raihan EVS 152<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>3 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>3 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Telescopic Sight VIV 160<br>1 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>3 Stormy Mountains EVS 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10 </strong><br>10 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=UeHaGtONyOE">TaloniteX’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1628214232126_151670" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1628214232126_151670"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Non-Competitive</p>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1628214232126_243564"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Vikavolt V and Sobble, evolving the Sobble into Drizzile as needed to make use of its Shady Dealings ability, which searches for any one Trainer card from your deck. Using Crushing Hammer, Fan of Waves, and Path to the Peak, cripple your opponent’s setup and power up Vikavolt V’s Paralyzing Bolt attack and use it to lock your opponent out of Item cards, further hampering their ability to setup.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/explosivewalker/025.shtml">Vikavolt V DAA 60</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/159/">Crushing Hammer SSH 159</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/infinityzone/090.shtml">Turbo Patch DAA 172</a> as a way to potentially get Vikavolt V’s Paralyzing Bolt powered up faster.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/160/">Cape of Toughness</a> to make Vikavolt V a bit more resilient against attacks.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/184/">Team Yell Grunt</a> as an additional Energy disruption option.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1628214232126_198273"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 15</strong><br>4 Sobble CRE 41<br>4 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>4 Vikavolt V DAA 60</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 34</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Bird Keeper DAA 159<br>4 Crushing Hammer SSH 159<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>1 Energy Search SSH 161<br>1 Fan of Waves BST 127<br>3 Path to the Peak CRE 148  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11</strong><br>4 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4<br>4 Speed Lightning Energy RCL 173<br>3 Capture Energy RCL 171</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_57316" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_57316"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Non-Competitive</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Blaziken V and Sylveon V and evolve them into Blaziken VMAX and Sylveon VMAX, respectively, also benching Zeraora V. In doing so, prepare to use Blaziken VMAX’s Max Blaze attack by discarding any extra Energy cards not needed to power the attack with Professor’s Research and Quick Ball. Setup knockouts and accelerate energy to benched Zeraora V using Blaziken VMAX’s Max Blaze attack. Then, when ready, use Zeraora V’s Cross Fist attack to snipe vulnerable multi-prize Pokémon from your opponent’s bench. Boost Zeraora V’s damage against Pokémon V with Passimian’s Throwing Coach ability. Use Sylveon VMAX as necessary to hit for weakness.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/matchlessfighter/008.shtml">Blaziken VMAX CRE 21</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/jetblackpoltergeist/024.shtml">Zeraora V CRE 53</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/matchlessfighter/043.shtml">Passimian CRE 88</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/140/">Rapid Strike Energy</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cre/152/">Rapid Strike Urshifu VMAX BST 88</a>. Alternatively, just play the <a href="#rsurshifuvmax">Rapid Strike Urshifu VMAX</a> archetype instead of this one.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1387441"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 22</strong><br>3 Blaziken V CRE 20<br>3 Blaziken VMAX CRE 21<br>3 Remoraid BST 36<br>3 Octillery BST 37<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>2 Passimian CRE 88<br>2 Sylveon V EVS 74<br>2 Sylveon VMAX EVS 75<br>2 Zeraora V CRE 53</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 28</strong><br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Great Ball CPA 52<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Escape Rope BST 125<br>2 Telescopic Sight VIV 160<br>1 Echoing Horn CRE 136<br>3 Tower of Waters BST 138</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10</strong><br>4 Rapid Strike Energy BST 140<br>4 Spiral Energy CRE 159<br>2 Capture Energy RCL 171</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_28829" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_28829"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Mareep into Flaaffy and use Boltund V’s Electrify attack and Flaaffy’s Dynamotor ability to get additional energy into play. Use Boltund V’s Bolt Storm to take knockouts, using Tapu Koko V primarily as a pivot to load up energy with Flaaffy’s Dynamotor ability, as necessary.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/67/">Boltund V RCL 67</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/27/">Flaaffy EVS 54</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/72/">Tapu Koko V SSH 72</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/51/">Tapu Koko VMAX BST 51</a> as a potential option against acceleration-heavy decks, like Shadow Rider Calyrex VMAX</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/48/">Zapdos VIV 48</a> as a finisher to KO benched Pokémon V (including Pokémon VMAX) after your opponent attempts to save them on the bench by retreating them or switching them out of the Active Spot.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1414965"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 15</strong><br>4 Boltund V RCL 67<br>4 Mareep DAA 55<br>4 Flaaffy EVS 55<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>1 Tapu Koko V SSH 72</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 33</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Zinnia's Resolve EVS 164<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Energy Switch SSH 162<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>3 Stormy Mountains EVS 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12 </strong><br>10 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4<br>2 Speed Lightning Energy RCL 173</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1625319975823_169641" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1625319975823_169641"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Non-Competitive</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Gardevoir V and, if going second, start with Cresselia in the Active Spot. Use Cresselia’s Cresecent Glow attack to pile Psychic Energy onto a benched Gardevoir V and evolve that Gardevoir V into the VMAX quickly to use its Max Cure attack, which does 180 damage and heals 50 damage from Gardevoir VMAX. Power up additional Gardevoir VMAX using Rose to attach two Psychic Energy from your discard pile.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CPA/17/">Gardevoir VMAX</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/168/">Rose</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/daa/169/">Rose Tower</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/155/">Memory Capsule</a> to make better use of <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cpa/16/">Gardevoir V</a>’s Swelling Pulse attack, which can do up to 200 damage if the Pokémon using the attack was healed during the turn it uses the attack.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/6/">Kricketune V</a> as an additional means of drawing out of the empty hands created by using Rose to power up Gardevoir VMAX.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1625319975823_194411"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Based on a pre-rotation <a href="https://play.limitlesstcg.com/tournament/60dacf495a6b7e0b0e262de6/player/jonnybrah/decklist">deck list by jonnybrah</a>.</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 11</strong><br>4 Gardevoir V CPA 16<br>3 Gardevoir VMAX CPA 17<br>3 Cresselia CRE 64<br>1 Galarian Articuno V CRE 58</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 37 </strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>4 Rose DAA 168<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Zinnia's Resolve EVS 164<br>4 Crushing Hammer SSH 159<br>4 Fog Crystal CRE 140<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Suspicious Food Tin CPA 66<br>1 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>1 Tool Scrapper RCL 168<br>4 Rose Tower DAA 169</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12 </strong><br>12 Psychic Energy SWSHEnergy 5</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_58934" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_58934"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <h2 style="padding-bottom:0px; margin-bottom:0px;">Malamar</h2>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1298002"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Inkay, Remoraid, and Minccino early on so that they can evolve into Malamar, Octillery, and Cinccino, respectively. Each turn, use Malamar’s Rapid Strike Tentacles attack, which does 40 damage for each Rapid Strike card you reveal from your hand, then shuffles those cards back into your deck. Refill you hand with new Rapid Strike cards each turn using Cinccino’s Made Do ability and Professor’s Research or, if Octillery’s Rapid Strike Search is available to seek it out from your deck, Korrina’s Focus.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/70/">Malamar CRE 70</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/37/">Octillery BST 37</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/128/">Korrina’s Focus</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">A single copy of <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CPA/44/">Inkay CPA 44</a> in place of one of the Inkay CRE 69 so that, with its extra 10 HP, it can survive Shadow Rider Calyrex V’s Astral Barrage attack. </p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1328922"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 21<br></strong>4 Inkay CRE 69<br>4 Malamar CRE 70<br>4 Remoraid BST 36<br>3 Octillery BST 37<br>3 Minccino SSH 145<br>3 Cinccino SSH 147</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32<br></strong>4 Korrina's Focus BST 128<br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Bruno BST 121<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Fog Crystal CRE 140<br>4 Great Ball CPA 52<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>2 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>2 Tower of Waters BST 138</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 7<br></strong>4 Spiral Energy CRE 159<br>3 Psychic Energy SWSHEnergy 5</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=MJ0u_3Tnyp8">ZapdosTCG’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_59609" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_59609"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts.   <h2 style="padding-bottom:0px; margin-bottom:0px;">Sylveon VMAX</h2>
  <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Minimal</p>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down the deck’s rainbow of Pokémon, powering up Sylveon VMAX in preparation for its Max Harmony attack, which does 70 damage plus thirty more damage for each type on Pokémon you have on your bench (for a maximum of 220 with a normal-sized bench of 5 Pokémon). Seek out useful Rapid Strike cards like Rapid Strike Energy, Korrina’s Focus, and Tower of Waters using Octillery’s Rapid Strike Search ability and recover energy and hit for weakness against Zacian V using Blaziken VMAX’s Max Blaze attack.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/41/">Sylveon VMAX EVS 75</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/65/">Ribbon Badge EVS 155</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/88/">Rapid Strike Urshifu VMAX BST 88</a> as an alternative attacker, sniper, and alternate Fighting-type representative to increase the damage of Sylveon VMAX’s Max Harmony attack.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/168/">Tool Scrapper</a> to remove problematic tools, like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/daa/160/">Cape of Toughness</a>.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1561329"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Blaziken V CRE 20<br>2 Blaziken VMAX CRE 21<br>3 Sylveon V EVS 74<br>3 Sylveon VMAX EVS 75<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>2 Remoraid BST 36<br>2 Octillery BST 37<br>1 Eldegoss V RCL 19<br>1 Oranguru SSH 148<br>1 Zeraora V CRE 53</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 28</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Korrina's Focus BST 128<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Ribbon Badge EVS 155<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125<br>3 Tower of Waters BST 138</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12</strong><br>4 Capture Energy RCL 171<br>4 Rapid Strike Energy BST 140<br>4 Spiral Energy CRE 159</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=pK5IXDPbihE">Tricky Gym’s version of this archetype</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=_4p1yEjLf8g">Tablemon’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_60486" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_60486"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. Embedded scripts are disabled while you're logged in and editing your site.&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeTitle&quot;:&quot;Embedded Scripts&quot;,&quot;exitSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Exit safe preview&quot;,&quot;exitSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;Please view the page after logging out for accurate rendering.&quot;,&quot;exitSafeModeTitle&quot;:&quot;Safe Preview&quot;,&quot;globalSafeMode&quot;:&quot;Embedded Code: This block contains embedded code that has been disabled.&quot;,&quot;scriptDisabled&quot;:&quot;Script Disabled&quot;}">

  <h2 style="padding-bottom:0px; margin-bottom:0px;">Dragapult</h2>
  <p style="font-size: 12px; padding-top:0px; margin-top: -4px;">Competitive Potential: Non-Competitive</p>
</div>
  
</div>
</div></div><div class="row sqs-row"><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block image-block sqs-block-image" data-block-type="5" data-sqsp-block="image-classic" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_699248"><div class="sqs-block-content">

Shin Nagasawa" elementtiming="system-image-block" data-sqsp-image-classic-block-image="" src="/images/401da393e53dd3af.webp" width="550" height="294" sizes="(max-width: 640px) 100vw, (max-width: 767px) 50vw, 50vw" style="display:block;object-fit: cover; width: 100%; height: 100%; object-position: 50% 50%" srcset="/images/401da393e53dd3af.webp 100w, /images/401da393e53dd3af.webp 300w, /images/401da393e53dd3af.webp 500w, /images/401da393e53dd3af.webp 750w, /images/401da393e53dd3af.webp 1000w, /images/401da393e53dd3af.webp 1500w, /images/401da393e53dd3af.webp 2500w" loading="lazy" decoding="async" data-loader="sqs">

            </div>

            <div class="image-caption"><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Illus. Shin Nagasawa</p></div>
          </figcaption>
        
      
        </figure>
      

    </div>
  


  


</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2790201"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Dreepy in the early game, working to get each of them evolved to a Dragapult as quickly as possible. It may be wise to avoid benching more than one or two, especially if it won’t be possible to guarantee evolution into Dragapult on the following turn—to avoid providing easy targets for Boss’s Orders. Spread damage using Dragapult’s Phantom Force attack, and the damage from Horror Psychic Energy, taking knockouts as they become available while trying to keep from slipping too far in the prize trade by flipping every heads you can on Dragapult’s Infiltrator ability.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/91/">Dragapult RCL 91</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/180/">Rare Candy SSH 180</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/172/">Horror Psychic Energy RCL 172</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/131/">Snorlax VIV 131</a> as an early-game option to draw additional cards.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1590092"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Spread damage using Dragapult VMAX’s Max Phantom attack and use Inteleon’s Quick Shooter ability and Horror Psychic Energy to help set up critical knock outs with Max Phantom. Use Drizzile’s Shady Dealings ability throughout the game to find critical Trainer cards.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S2/50/">Dragapult VMAX RCL 93</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S2/95/">Horror Psychic Energy RCL 172</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="http://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/148/">Path to the Peak</a> both to slow your opponent’s setup and to make it more difficult for them to recover from the energy disruption of Crushing Hammer and Fan of Waves</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/12/">Galarian Articuno EVS 63</a> in combination with a higher count of basic Psychic Energy and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/162/">Energy Switch</a> to make it easier to power up Dragapult VMAX sooner, especially after one is knocked out.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1619244"><div class="sqs-block-content">

</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Shadow Rider Calyrex V into Shadow Rider Calyrex VMAX to make full use of its Underworld Door ability, which not only attaches a Psychic Energy from your hand to one of your benched Psychic Pokémon, it draws two cards when you do. Use either Shadow Rider Calyrex VMAX’s Max Geist attack or Alcremie VMAX’s G-Max Whisk attack to take knockouts.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/75/">Shadow Rider Calyrex VMAX CRE 75</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cpa/23/">Alcremie VMAX CPA 23</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/15/">Golurk V EVS 70</a> to make use of Rewind Beam to devolve high-HP evolved Pokémon and by so doing take a knock out against their lower-evolution.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1628608245679_154580" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1628608245679_154580"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. </div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2858201"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Setup Coalossal V and Oranguru early, then evolve into Coalossal VMAX as soon as possible. Use Oranguru’s Primate Wisdom ability and Rotom Phone to guarantee that the top card of your deck is an energy to maximize damage from Coalossal VMAX’s first attack, Eruption Blast. As powered-up, use its second attack to take devastating knockouts. Use Drizzile’s Shady Dealings ability throughout the game to find critical Trainer cards.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/99">Coalossal VMAX VIV 99</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/148/">Oranguru SSH 148</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSP/43/">Galarian Sirfetch’d V SWSH043</a> to take advantage of its Resolute Spear ability and its Meteor Smash attack to finish off damaged opposing Pokémon.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1718996"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 17</strong><br>3 Coalossal V VIV 98<br>3 Coalossal VMAX VIV 99<br>3 Sobble CRE 41<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon SSH 58<br>2 Oranguru SSH 148<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 31</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Cheryl BST 123<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Rotom Phone CPA 64<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>1 Energy Search SSH 161<br>1 Pal Pad SSH 172  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12</strong><br>8 Fighting Energy SWSHEnergy 6<br>4 Stone Fighting Energy VIV 164</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_796579" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_796579"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Use Level Ball and Gossifleur’s Call for Family attack to fish out Silicobra from your deck and place them on your bench. Then, using Peony and Evolution Incense, find your Sandaconda and evolve your Silicobra. Continue to remove non-Energy cards from your deck with Peony. Use Sandaconda’s Big Sand Cannon to attack, recycling energy, Silicobra, and Sandaconda as necessary using Ordinary Rod and Energy Recycler.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/rapidstrikemaster/048.shtml">Sandaconda BST 82</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/150/">Peony</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/145/">Klara</a>, in place of Energy Recycler, Ordinary Rod, or both, as a means of recovering both Pokémon and Energy cards.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1755288"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 12 </strong><br>4 Silicobra SSH 107<br>4 Sandaconda BST 82<br>3 Gossifleur SSH 20<br>1 Kricketune V BST 6</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 22 </strong><br>4 Peony CRE 150<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Pokégear 3.0 SSH 174<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Energy Recycler GRI 123<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 26 </strong><br>26 Fighting Energy SWSHEnergy 6</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_30207" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623501056744_30207"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Sandaconda V and Sobble on your first turn, attacking using Sandaconda V’s Sand Eater attack if you go second. Evolve into Sandaconda VMAX and attack using its G-Max Cyclone attack to take knockouts against your opponent’s Active Pokémon. When your Sandaconda VMAX is at risk of being knocked out on your opponent’s next turn, use G-Max Cyclone’s effect to move off energy onto your benched Sandaconda V or VMAX. Use Cheryl to heal as necessary. Use Sandaconda VMAX’s Sand Pulse attack in conjunction with Telescopic Sight to spread damage to your opponent’s benched Pokémon V (including Pokémon VMAX).</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/silverlance/044.shtml">Sandaconda VMAX CRE 90</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/148/">Path to the Peak</a> both to slow your opponent’s setup and to hinder their recovery after knockouts.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1797548"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">

</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2927641"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Set up by benching both Sobble and Galarian Farfetch’d, preparing to evolve into Galarian Sirfetch’d at the earliest opportunity. Attach to the Galarian Farfetch’d turn one, then attach either the missing Fighting or missing Twin Energy turn two. Use the deck’s variety of switching options to reset Galarian Sirfetch’d’s Meteor Assault attack when necessary. Evolve Sobble into Drizzile through the game to take advantage of its Shady Dealings ability, which allows you to search your deck for any one Trainer card and put it into your hand.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S1a/43/">Galarian Sirfetch’d RCL 95</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/148/">Path to the Peak</a> both to slow your opponent’s setup and to hinder their recovery after knockouts.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSP/43/">Galarian Sirfetch’d V SWSH043</a> to take advantage of its Resolute Spear ability and its Meteor Smash attack to finish off damaged opposing Pokémon.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1829355"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 16</strong><br>4 Galarian Farfetch'd RCL 94<br>4 Galarian Sirfetch'd RCL 95<br>3 Sobble CRE 41<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 33</strong><br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Bird Keeper DAA 159<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Piers CPA 58<br>1 Raihan EVS 152<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Switch SSH 183<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>2 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>1 Energy Search SSH 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11</strong><br>7 Fighting Energy SWSHEnergy 6<br>4 Twin Energy RCL 174</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_81399" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_81399"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Houndour and Galarian Farfetch’d into Houndoom and Galarian Sirfetch’d, respectively. Use Houndoom’s Single Strike Roar to attack Single Strike Energy to Galarian Sirfetch’d and attach Twin Energy to fully power-up its Leek Strike attack, which does 70 damage plus 90 damage if it has a Pokémon Tool card attached to it.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S5a/36/">Galarian Sirfetch’d CRE 79</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/96/">Houndoom BST 96</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/141/">Single Strike Energy</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Fighting Energy and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/63/">Raihan</a>, as a means both to quickly accelerate the required Fighting Energy and as a means of searching out Twin Energy from the deck.</p></li></ul>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Stonjourner V into Stonjourner VMAX, healing it with its Stone Gift attack or with Cheryl, as necessary, accelerating energy to it via Galarian Zapdos and Energy Switch. As the opportunity presents, attack with Stonjourner VMAX’s Max Rockfall to take knockouts.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/116/">Stonjourner VMAX SSH 116</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/166/">Hyper Potion</a> as another means of healing off damage.</p></li></ul>
</div>

</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Evolve Rapid Strike Urshifu V into Rapid Strike Urshifu VMAX and use the deck’s various switching cards to hit your opponent’s Pokémon with Gale Thrust, taking selective knockouts against supporting Pokémon like Crobat V and Kricketune V where possible to take advantage of weakness. Use Galarian Moltres V’s Direflame Wings ability in conjunction with Energy Search to chain G-Max Rapid Flow to take knockouts on your opponent’s benched Pokémon. Boost Rapid Strike Urshifu VMAX’s damage against benched Pokémon V with Telescopic Sight and with Passimian’s Throwing Coach ability.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/battlestyles/088.shtml">Rapid Strike Urshifu VMAX BST 88</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/97/">Galarian Moltres V CRE 97</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/162/">Energy Switch</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SHF/50/">Ditto V SHF 50</a> as, effectively, an extra Rapid Strike Urshifu V or Galarian Moltres V, as needed.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/36/">Medicham V EVS 83</a> to take advantage of its Yoga Loop attack, gaining an additional turn when the two damage counters it drops cause your opponent’s Pokémon to be knocked out.</p></li></ul>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_3017398"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Get out Houndour and Single Strike Urshifu V on early turns so that they can evolve as soon as possible. Using Houndoom's Single Strike Roar ability, search your deck for Single Strike Energy and attach them to Single Strike Urshifu VMAX then attack using its Beatdown attack. As is necessary, take knockouts using its G-Max One Blow attack and recycle your Single Strike Energy using Urn of Vitality. When Single Strike Urshifu VMAX is adequately damaged, also take advantage of Single Strike Scroll of Scorn to use the scroll's additional attack, Furious Anger</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/battlestyles/086.shtml">Single Strike Urshifu VMAX BST 86</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/battlestyles/096.shtml">Houndoom BST 96</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S5I/64/">Single Strike Scroll of Scorn BST 133</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/singlestrikemaster/070.shtml">Single Strike Energy BST 141</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/48/">Umbreon VMAX EVS 95</a> as an alternative gusting option.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_1984495"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Source: <a href="https://twitter.com/JoeyREDSs/status/1382154182690492416">Anthony Alvarez (via Joey G)</a></p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18</strong><br>4 Houndour BST 95<br>4 Houndoom BST 96<br>3 Single Strike Urshifu V BST 85<br>3 Single Strike Urshifu VMAX BST 86<br>2 Tyranitar V BST 97<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32 </strong><br>4 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Urn of Vitality BST 139<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Single Strike Scroll of Scorn BST 133<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>1 Tower of Darkness BST 137</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10 </strong><br>4 Single Strike Energy BST 141<br>3 Capture Energy RCL 171<br>3 Fighting Energy SWSHEnergy 6</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=RKG5VvurS6U">Rahul Reddy’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_86313" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_86313"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Pile on damage using Galarian Zigzagoon’s Headbutt Tantrum ability and Flapple’s Apple Drop ability. Attack using either Galarian Moltres V with its Aura Burn ability or, once enough damage has been piled onto the target, using Sableye V and its Crazy Claws attack. Drop in Sableye V’s right before use where possible, using tricks like Galarian Moltres V Direflame Wings ability and Energy Switch to get the energy necessary to use Crazy Claws immediately after Sableye V comes into play.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/120/">Sableye V SSH 120</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/22/">Flapple RCL 22</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/97/">Galarian Moltres V CRE 97</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/162/">Energy Switch</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/63/">Raihan</a> as an additional means of powering up a Sableye V and as a universal search option after one of your Pokémon has been knocked out.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2052879"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18</strong><br>4 Applin RCL 21<br>4 Flapple RCL 22<br>4 Sableye V SSH 120<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>2 Galarian Moltres V CRE 97<br>2 Galarian Zigzagoon SSH 117  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32 </strong><br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Energy Switch SSH 162<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Exp. Share BST 126<br>2 Great Ball CPA 52<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>1 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>3 Turffield Stadium CPA 68  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10 </strong><br>8 Darkness Energy SWSHEnergy 7<br>2 Hiding Darkness Energy DAA 175</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Malamar V and evolve it into Malamar VMAX when possible. Meanwhile, bench Galarian Moltres V’s Direflame Wings ability to also get additional energy into play. Use Energy Switch to move Darkness Energy from your Pokémon to where it is needed. Disrupt your opponent’s hand with Thievul’s Fumbling Hands ability, which causes both players to shuffle their hands, put them on the bottom of their deck, then draw 4 cards. Attack with Malamar VMAX’s Max Jammer attack which, in addition to dealing 180 damage, allows you to pick a card from your opponent’s hand to put at the bottom of their deck.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/122/">Malamar VMAX RCL 122</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/97/">Galarian Moltres V CRE 97</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/162/">Energy Switch</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/61/">Spirit Mask</a> as a bit of additional hand disruption.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1627044071775_256406"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>3 Galarian Moltres V CRE 97<br>3 Malamar V RCL 121<br>3 Malamar VMAX RCL 122<br>3 Nickit SSH 125<br>3 Thievul EVS 105<br>2 Galarian Moltres EVS 93<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>1 Eldegoss V RCL 19</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 29</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Crushing Hammer SSH 159<br>4 Energy Switch SSH 162<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>3 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11</strong><br>8 Fighting Energy SWSHEnergy 6<br>3 Hiding Darkness Energy DAA 175</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_87013" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_87013"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down available Pokémon to fill the bench by the end of your second turn, using the deck’s search options and Crobat V to help draw you find additional Pokémon by drawing cards. Attach Darkness Energy as available to power up and attack using Eternatus VMAX’s <em>Dread End</em>. Get additional Energy when needed using Galarian Moltres V’s <em>Direflame Wings </em>ability and Energy Switch. Choose which of your opponent’s Pokémon to knock out by gusting via Boss’s Orders or Umbreon VMAX’s Dark Signal ability.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/infinityzone/065.shtml">Eternatus VMAX DAA 117</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://serebii.net/card/infinityzone/053.shtml">Crobat V DAA 104</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/48/">Umbreon VMAX EVS 95</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/147/">Old Cemetery</a> or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/137/">Tower of Darkness</a> as a counter to the hostile stadium <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/148/">Path to the Peak</a>.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/104/">Liepard CRE 104</a> as a tool-countering option, especially to get rid of pesky HP-boosting or damage reducing tools like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/158/">Big Charm</a> and <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/daa/160/">Cape of Toughness</a>.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/130/">Phoebe</a> to cut through walling Pokémon, like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Decidueye DAA 13</a>.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2106353"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 20</strong><br>4 Crobat V DAA 104<br>4 Eternatus V DAA 116<br>4 Eternatus VMAX DAA 117<br>3 Umbreon V EVS 94<br>2 Umbreon VMAX EVS 95<br>2 Galarian Moltres V CRE 97<br>1 Hoopa DAA 111</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 30 </strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>4 Energy Switch SSH 162<br>4 Great Ball CPA 52<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Switch SSH 183<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10 </strong><br>8 Darkness Energy SWSHEnergy 7<br>2 Hiding Darkness Energy DAA 175</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=xpnuqs0lJm0">ZapdosTCG’s version of this archetype</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=3l6-hOd7Zc8">TrainerChip’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench and evolve Houndour and Kubfu into Houndoom and Single Strike Urshifu, respectively. Use Houndoom’s Single Strike Roar to attack Single Strike Energy to Single Strike Urshifu and manually attach energy as necessary to fully power-up its Fists of Strike attack, which does 100 damage plus 100 damage if Single Strike Urshifu has any damage counters on it.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S5a/55/">Single Strike Urshifu CRE 108</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/96/">Houndoom BST 96</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/141/">Single Strike Energy</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/skyscrapingperfect/041.shtml">Dialga EVS 112</a> as a stalling option against Pokémon V, including Pokémon VMAX.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2154230"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18</strong><br>4 Houndour BST 95<br>4 Houndoom BST 96<br>4 Kubfu CRE 93<br>4 Single Strike Urshifu CRE 108<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104<br>1 Stonjourner BST 84</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32 </strong><br>4 Bruno BST 121<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Zinnia's Resolve EVS 164<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Urn of Vitality BST 139<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>4 Tower of Darkness BST 137</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 10 </strong><br>4 Single Strike Energy BST 141<br>4 Capture Energy RCL 171<br>2 Darkness Energy SWSHEnergy 7</p>
