<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Bronzor and Corviknight V and evolve them as early as possible. Attach energy with manual attachments and with Metal Saucer, then rearrange where the energy is attached using Bronzong's Metal Transfer ability. Attack using Corviknight VMAX's G-Max Hurricane and using Cheryl to heal off damage from Corviknight VMAX after removing any energy from it using Metal Transfer to avoid losing any attached energy.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/138/">Zacian V SSH 138</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/rapidstrikemaster/056.shtml">Corviknight VMAX BST 110</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/singlestrikemaster/050.shtml">Bronzong BST 102</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/rapidstrikemaster/068.shtml">Cheryl</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/126/">Aegislash V VIV 126</a> to cut through walling Pokémon, like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Decidueye DAA 13</a>.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Zamazenta V SSH 139</a> as a wall against Pokémon VMAX, to slow them down as necessary.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2189499"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18</strong><br>4 Bronzor BST 101<br>3 Bronzong BST 102<br>4 Corviknight V BST 109<br>3 Corviknight VMAX BST 110<br>4 Zacian V SSH 138</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 29</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>3 Cheryl BST 123<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>4 Metal Saucer SSH 170<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Switch SSH 183<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>1 Air Balloon SSH 156</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 13</strong><br>10 Metal Energy SWSHEnergy 8<br>3 Coating Metal Energy VIV 163</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_89401" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_89401"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Bronzor and Cufant and evolve them as early as possible. Attach energy with manual attachments and with Metal Saucer, then rearrange where the energy is attached using Bronzong's Metal Transfer ability. Attack using Zacian V’s Brave Blade and Copperajah's Muscular Nose attack and use the deck’s switching cards to reset Zacian V’s attack, as necessary.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/138/">Zacian V SSH 138</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/137/">Copperajah SSH 137</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/singlestrikemaster/050.shtml">Bronzong BST 102</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/126/">Aegislash V VIV 126</a> to cut through walling Pokémon, like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Decidueye DAA 13</a>.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Zamazenta V SSH 139</a> as a wall against Pokémon VMAX, to slow them down as necessary.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/158/">Big Charm</a> to make it more difficult for decks that float around the 170-190 damage output range to take knockouts against Copperajah.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/123/">Cheryl</a> to heal off damage from your Copperajah, punishing your opponent when they fail to take a knockout.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2225312"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 16</strong><br>4 Cufant SSH 136<br>3 Copperajah SSH 137<br>3 Bronzor BST 101<br>3 Bronzong BST 102<br>3 Zacian V SSH 138</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 31</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Metal Saucer SSH 170<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>4 Switch SSH 183<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 13</strong><br>10 Metal Energy SWSHEnergy 8<br>3 Coating Metal Energy VIV 163</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Sobble and Zacian V and attach energy to Zacian V via manual attachments, Metal Saucer, and Zacian V’s ability, Intrepid Sword. Over the course of the game, evolve Sobble into Drizzile to take advantage of its Shady Dealings ability, which allows you to search the deck for any Trainer card you may need. Evolve Drizzile into Inteleon CRE to make use of its Quick Shooting ability to put additional damage on Pokémon to make them easier to knock out with Zacian V’s Brave Blade attack.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/138/">Zacian V SSH 138</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/126/">Aegislash V VIV 126</a> to cut through walling Pokémon, like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Decidueye DAA 13</a>.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/viv/154/">Leon</a> and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SHF/62/">Rusted Sword</a> as an additional means of boosting the damage of Zacian V’s Brave Blade attack when necessary.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/66/">Crystal Cave</a> in place of <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/150/">Circhester Bath</a> to heal off damage from each of your Zacian V.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2362686"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 14</strong><br>4 Sobble CRE 41<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>2 Inteleon CRE 43<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>4 Zacian V SSH 138</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 35</strong><br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>1 Bird Keeper DAA 159<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Metal Saucer SSH 170<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>4 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Cape of Toughness DAA 160<br>2 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>1 Energy Search SSH 161<br>1 Escape Rope BST 125<br>2 Circhester Bath VIV 150</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 11</strong><br>11 Metal Energy SWSHEnergy 8</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=6qpcegTL8jM">Tricky Gym’s version of this archetype</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://twitter.com/PeterJoltik/status/1424160280884523011/photo/1">Peter Kica’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Sobble and Zamazenta V and attach energy to Zamazenta V via manual attachments and Metal Saucer. Over the course of the game, evolve Sobble into Drizzile to take advantage of its Shady Dealings ability, which allows you to search the deck for any Trainer card you may need. Evolve Drizzile into Inteleon CRE to make use of its Quick Shooting ability to put additional damage on Pokémon to make them easier to knock out with Zamazenta V’s Assault Tackle attack, which discards a Special Energy card attached to your opponent’s Active Pokémon. Continue walling Pokémon VMAX via Zamazenta’s Dauntless Shield ability, buffing Zamazenta V’s effective HP with Rusted Shield and Circhester Bath or healing it as needed with Crystal Cave. In matchups where Zamazenta V’s damage output or wall capabilities are inadequate, use Zacian V’s Brave Blade attack to take knockouts.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/139/">Zamazenta V SSH 139</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/138/">Zacian V SSH 138</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/126/">Aegislash V VIV 126</a> to cut through walling Pokémon, like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Decidueye DAA 13</a>.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1629934129742_217137"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">

</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Play down Dragonite V and Snorunt, and over the course of the game, play down and evolve Sobble into Drizzile to take advantage of its Shady Dealings ability, which allows you to search for any 1 Trainer card from your deck. Use a combination of Melony and the on-evolution effect of Froslass’ Frost Over ability to power up Dragonite V’s Dragon Gale attack, which does 250 damage to your opponent’s Active Pokémon, but also does 20 damage to each of your benched Pokémon. As necessary, use Suicune V and its Blizzard Rondo attack to finish off or soften up your opponent’s Pokémon.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/42/">Dragonite V EVS 191</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/56/">Drizzile SSH 56</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/36/">Froslass CRE 36</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/50/">Flapple EVS 120</a> or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/51/">Appletun EVS 121</a> as alternate attackers that deal 50 damage for each Pokémon with an ability and 70 damage for each Special Energy attached to your opponent’s Pokémon, respectively.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/63/">Raihan</a> as a means of Energy recovery and as a universal search option after one of your Pokémon is knocked out.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2414314"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 16</strong><br>4 Dragonite V EVS 191<br>3 Sobble CRE 41<br>3 Drizzile SSH 56<br>1 Inteleon SSH 58<br>2 Snorunt CRE 35<br>2 Froslass CRE 36<br>1 Suicune V EVS 31</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 32</strong><br>4 Melony CRE 146<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Marnie CPA 56<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Scoop Up Net RCL 165<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>3 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Capacious Bucket RCL 156<br>2 Energy Search SSH 161<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>1 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>1 Ordinary Rod SSH 171</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12 </strong><br>8 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3<br>4 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Basic Strategy</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Get Rayquaza V and Mareep into play as early as possible, evolving into Rayquaza VMAX and Flaaffy as the opportunity presents. Manually attach Fire energy to Rayquaza VMAX and use Flaaffy’s Dynamotor ability or Rose, as necessary, to attach additional energy to Rayquaza VMAX. Attack using Rayquaza VMAX’s Max Burst attack, discarding Lightning energy attached to the attacking Rayquaza VMAX to do 80 damage for each Lightning Energy discarded.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Key Cards</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/46/">Rayquaza V EVS 110</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/47/">Rayquaza VMAX 111</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/27/">Flaaffy EVS 55</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/168/">Rose DAA 168</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Other Considerations</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/rcl/67/">Boltund V RCL 67</a> as an alternate attacker and additional means of energy acceleration.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/daa/60/">Vikavolt V DAA 60</a> as an alternate attacker that can also be used to cripple your opponent’s setup or recovery.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/138/">Tower of Waters</a> as an additional retreating option and counter to the hostile stadium, <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/cre/148/">Path to the Peak</a>.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/166/">Pokémon Breeder’s Nurturing</a> as an additional out to evolving Mareep into Flaaffy and Rayquaza V into Rayquaza VMAX.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_76337"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 17<br></strong>4 Mareep DAA 55<br>3 Flaaffy EVS 55<br>3 Rayquaza V EVS 110<br>3 Rayquaza VMAX EVS 111<br>2 Snorlax VIV 131<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104<br>1 Zeraora V CRE 53</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 31<br></strong>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Rose DAA 168<br>3 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>3 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>3 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Level Ball BST 129<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>1 Energy Search SSH 161<br>3 Stormy Mountains EVS 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12 <br></strong>8 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4<br>4 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=h_iBZPVeJP4">OmniPoke’s version of this archetype</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=4VrAVpuykow">Rare Candy’s version of this archetype</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=NbmXb6bK6jg">ZapdosTCG’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_91154" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1623497508274_91154"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Bronzor and Duraludon V and evolve them as early as possible. Attach energy with manual attachments and with Metal Saucer, then rearrange where the energy is attached using Bronzong's Metal Transfer ability. Attack using Zacian V’s Brave Blade or Duraludon VMAX's G-Max Pulverization and use Cheryl to heal off damage from Duraludon VMAX after removing any energy from it using Metal Transfer to avoid losing any attached energy.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/138/">Zacian V SSH 138</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/49/">Duraludon VMAX EVS 123</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/singlestrikemaster/050.shtml">Bronzong BST 102</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/rapidstrikemaster/068.shtml">Cheryl</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/126/">Aegislash V VIV 126</a> to cut through walling Pokémon, like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Decidueye DAA 13</a>.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Zamazenta V SSH 139</a> as a wall against Pokémon VMAX, to slow them down as necessary.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2311689"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 19</strong><br>4 Duraludon V CPA 47<br>3 Duraludon VMAX EVS 123<br>4 Zacian V SSH 138<br>3 Bronzor RCL 129<br>2 Bronzong BST 102<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>1 Galarian Zapdos V CRE 80</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 28</strong><br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Cheryl BST 123<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Metal Saucer SSH 170<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Switch SSH 183</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 13</strong><br>9 Metal Energy SWSHEnergy 8<br>4 Aurora Energy SSH 186</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=WZi-QnzSsz8">Tablemon’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1625013205806_207437" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1625013205806_207437"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. <div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Regidrago and Cherubi, and evolve Cherubi into Cherrim as early as possible. Attach Exp. Share and Fire Energy to Regidrago in anticipation of using Cherrim’s Spring Bloom ability to fill in the Grass Energy requirement of Regidrago’s Dragon Energy attack, which you’ll use to chain knockouts.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/52/">Regidrago EVS 124</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/8/">Cherrim BST 8</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/4/">Tropius EVS 6</a> to make use of its Rally Back attack to deal an easy 120 damage after one of your other Pokémon has been knocked out.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/50/">Flapple EVS 120</a> or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7R/51/">Appletun EVS 121</a> as alternate attackers that deal 50 damage for each Pokémon with an ability and 70 damage for each Special Energy attached to your opponent’s Pokémon, respectively.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/16/">Tapu Bulu BST 16</a> as an alternate attacker, taking advantage of its Nature’s Judgement attack, which can fairly easily deal 160 damage.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/BST/121/">Bruno</a> as an alternate draw Supporter—one that can draw you 7 cards after one of your Pokémon is knocked out.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/CRE/145/">Klara</a> as an alternate means of Pokémon and Energy recovery.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/63/">Raihan</a> as a means of Energy recovery and as a universal search option after one of your Pokémon is knocked out.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/165/">Scoop Up Net</a> as both a pseudo-switch and pseudo-healing card for Regidrago, especially, to clear off any damage that may be hampering its Dragon Energy attack’s damage output.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1625013205806_232292"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 13</strong><br>4 Regidrago EVS 124<br>3 Cherubi BST 7<br>3 Cherrim BST 8<br>2 Jirachi VIV 119<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 34</strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Marnie CPA 56<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Exp. Share BST 126<br>3 Switch SSH 183<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Energy Retrieval SSH 160<br>2 Energy Search SSH 161<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>3 Stormy Mountains EVS 161</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 13</strong><br>9 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1<br>4 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Alternate Lists</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=B5oy3o3QKF4">Rare Candy’s version of this archetype</a></p></li></ul>
</div>

</div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_3268205"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Build up your board by playing down Gossifleur and using its Call for Family attack if you go second to pull out and bench, for an example, additional Gossifleur, Smeargle, or Jirachi from your deck. Evolve Gossifleur into Eldegoss and use its Cotton Lift ability to get as many different types of basic Energy into your hand as you can prior to attacking with Smeargle’s Live Painting attack, which does 30 damage plus 30 more damage for each type of basic Energy you reveal from your hand. As necessary, use Raihan to attach a basic Energy card from your discard pile to a fresh Smeagle after one has been knocked out, then attach another from your hand to finish powering up its Live Painting attack—or alternatively use Raihan to search for a Twin Energy and attach that to Smeargle instead.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/59/">Smeagle EVS 128</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S6a/10/">Eldegoss EVS 16</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/RCL/169/">Training Court</a> to recover basic Energy cards back to your hand to boost the damage of Smeargle’s Live Painting attack.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/ssh/183/">Switch</a> and/or <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/159/">Bird Keeper</a> as switching options to help get a Gossifleur, Eldegoss, Crobat V, or Jirachi out of the Active Spot.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/daa/165/">Piers</a> to search out both Crobat V and Twin Energy.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/rcl/165/">Scoop Up Net</a> both as a switching option for most of the deck’s Pokémon and as a potential option to make double use of Eldegoss’ Cotton Lift by evolving a different Gossifleur with the scooped-up Eldegoss.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/bst/124/">Energy Recycler</a> to recover discarded Energy cards in the mid to late game.</p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/161/">Energy Search</a> to help find the necessary Energy cards necessary to take knockouts against your opponent’s Pokémon.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2473221"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 15 </strong><br>4 Smeargle EVS 128<br>4 Gossifleur SSH 20<br>3 Eldegoss EVS 16<br>2 Crobat V DAA 104<br>2 Jirachi VIV 119</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 33 </strong><br>4 Bruno BST 121<br>4 Raihan EVS 152<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Zinnia's Resolve EVS 164<br>1 Klara CRE 145<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>3 Pokégear 3.0 SSH 174<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>2 Lucky Egg SSH 167<br>2 Ordinary Rod SSH 171<br>1 Energy Retrieval SSH 160<br>2 Turffield Stadium CPA 68</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 12 </strong><br>4 Twin Energy RCL 174<br>1 Grass Energy SWSHEnergy 1<br>1 Fire Energy SWSHEnergy 2<br>1 Water Energy SWSHEnergy 3<br>1 Lightning Energy SWSHEnergy 4<br>1 Psychic Energy SWSHEnergy 5<br>1 Fighting Energy SWSHEnergy 6<br>1 Darkness Energy SWSHEnergy 7<br>1 Metal Energy SWSHEnergy 8</p>
</div>

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Bench Bronzor and Togekiss V and evolve them as early as possible. Attach energy with manual attachments and with Metal Saucer, then rearrange where the energy is attached using Bronzong's Metal Transfer ability. Attack using Zacian V’s Brave Blade or Togekiss VMAX's Max Glide and use Cheryl to heal off damage from Togekiss VMAX after removing any energy from it using Metal Transfer to avoid losing any attached energy.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/SSH/138/">Zacian V SSH 138</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/141/">Togekiss VMAX VIV 141</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/singlestrikemaster/050.shtml">Bronzong BST 102</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/rapidstrikemaster/068.shtml">Cheryl</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/VIV/126/">Aegislash V VIV 126</a> to cut through walling Pokémon, like <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/DAA/13/">Decidueye DAA 13</a>.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2511526"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 18</strong><br>4 Togekiss V VIV 140<br>3 Togekiss VMAX VIV 141<br>1 Crobat V DAA 104<br>3 Bronzor RCL 129<br>2 Bronzong BST 102<br>3 Zacian V SSH 138<br>1 Zamazenta V SSH 139<br>1 Ditto V SHF 50</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 29</strong><br>4 Marnie CPA 56<br>3 Cheryl BST 123<br>3 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>4 Metal Saucer SSH 170<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>2 Big Charm SSH 158<br>2 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>2 Escape Rope BST 125<br>2 Switch SSH 183<br>1 Pal Pad SSH 172</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 13</strong><br>9 Metal Energy SWSHEnergy 8<br>4 Capture Energy RCL 171</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div><div class="sqs-block website-component-block sqs-block-website-component sqs-block-code code-block" data-block-css="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.styles.css&quot;]" data-block-scripts="[&quot;https://definitions.sqspcdn.com/website-component-definition/static-assets/website.components.code/931cecc8-b0d3-487f-aa41-bbf557a88c0d_663/website.components.code.visitor.js&quot;]" data-block-type="1337" data-definition-name="website.components.code" data-sqsp-block="code" data-website-component-id="yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_875486" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_875486"><div class="sqs-block-content"><div class="sqs-code-container" data-localized="{&quot;enableSafeModeButton&quot;:&quot;Preview in safe mode&quot;,&quot;enableSafeModeText&quot;:&quot;This block contains embedded scripts. </div></div><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_3309647"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">BASIC STRATEGY</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Set up by benching Sinistea on early turns, discarding other Pokémon with the Mad Party attack. Evolve Sinistea to Polteageist and, using Triple Acceleration Energy or Twin Energy to power up Polteageist’s Mad Party attack, take knockouts against your opponent’s Pokémon. Discard additional Mad Party Pokémon using Polteageist’s Tea Break ability and Cinccino’s Make Do ability, which both also draw additional cards. On turns after your Polteageist have been knocked out, use the deck’s various Pokémon Search cards to find Bunnelby and attack using its Mad Party attack. Recover from knockouts using Raihan to attach Psychic energy from your Discard pile and then use it, if necessary, to retrieve another Psychic energy or Twin Energy, as appropriate, to power up your follow-up attack.</p><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">KEY CARDS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/explosivewalker/058.shtml">Bunnelby DAA 150</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/explosivewalker/019.shtml">Galarian Mr. Rime DAA 36</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/explosivewalker/033.shtml">Dedenne DAA 78</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://www.serebii.net/card/explosivewalker/037.shtml">Polteageist DAA 83</a></p></li><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/63/">Raihan</a></p></li></ul><h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">OTHER CONSIDERATIONS</h3><ul data-rte-list="default"><li><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;">A fourth copy of <a href="https://limitlesstcg.com/cards/jp/S7D/63/">Raihan</a> to ensure it is available as needed.</p></li></ul>
</div>






















</div></div></div><div class="col sqs-col-6 span-6"><div class="sqs-block html-block sqs-block-html" data-block-type="2" data-sqsp-block="text" id="block-yui_3_17_2_1_1624375080311_2557714"><div class="sqs-block-content">

<div class="sqs-html-content" data-sqsp-text-block-content="">
  <h3 style="white-space:pre-wrap;">Example Deck List</h3><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Pokémon - 24</strong><br>4 Bunnelby DAA 150<br>4 Dedenne DAA 78<br>4 Galarian Mr. Rime DAA 36<br>4 Polteageist DAA 83<br>3 Minccino SSH 145<br>3 Cinccino SSH 147<br>2 Sinistea DAA 82</p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Trainer Cards - 27 </strong><br>4 Professor's Research CPA 62<br>3 Raihan EVS 152<br>2 Boss's Orders RCL 154<br>2 Marnie CPA 56<br>1 Zinnia's Resolve EVS 164<br>4 Evolution Incense SSH 163<br>4 Level Ball BST 129<br>4 Quick Ball SSH 179<br>2 Air Balloon SSH 156<br>1 Rescue Carrier EVS 154  </p><p class="" style="white-space:pre-wrap;"><strong>Energy - 9 </strong><br>5 Psychic Energy SWSHEnergy 5<br>4 Twin Energy RCL 174</p>
</div>






















</div></div></div></div></div></div>
